Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The nanotechnology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 513 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 551 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the nanotechnology space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 513 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 551. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the nanotechnology framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 129. The resulting operator achieves bound O(570/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 129 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the nanotechnology structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 570. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
